- While the repository is public, no row, feature table or model weight goes into a commit, a workflow log or an artifact (RFC §2). Switch `MODEL_DATASET_MODE` (`on` by default, `off` records nothing). It never feeds a room, a decision or Omega.

### Strategy agents (owner 2026-11-13, `docs/RFC_STRATEGY_AGENTS.md`, `src/strategy-agents.mjs`)
- Goal (owner): "kita perlu agent yang bisa dibongkar pasang menyesuaikan strategy yang tepat yang dipilih oleh council". A strategy agent is one way of trading price action behind one interface (`{ id, version, label, fits, plan(dir, ctx) }`); the registry `STRATEGY_AGENTS` holds them, and adding or removing one is one entry.
- **An agent's plan** is pure and deterministic: from the price, the 1H ATR, the Key Level Agent's zones, the closed 15m bars, the 1H regime and the Projection Agent's chart patterns with their measured record on gold, its own entry, stop and targets in the shape of the engine's plan, or null when its setup is not on the chart. It never reads a room, a witness or the quorum and never decides the side. A stop is at least 0.25 ATR away; more than 2.5 ATR of risk is no plan.
- **Agents:** `zone_pullback` (the strategy engine's plan of before, `planForDirection`); `sweep_reversal` (a new 3-hour extreme through a key zone within three 15m bars, closed back inside: enter at the close, the stop 0.1 ATR beyond the sweep); `breakout_retest` (a 15m close 0.1 ATR beyond a zone that held price for three bars, within six bars and not closed back through: enter at the broken edge, the stop behind the zone or the breakout bar); `range_fade` (a ranging 1H regime, price in the outer 35 % of its 8-hour range of 1 to 4 ATR: enter at the edge, the stop 0.25 ATR beyond it, the targets the middle and the far side); and eight **pattern families** (below).
- **Pattern families** (owner 2026-11-13: "libatkan Projection Agent karena dia yang punya knowledge terkait price action"; the 53 chart patterns and 10 event patterns of its catalogue, "benar"): one agent per family, `pattern_<family>`, trading the patterns of that family the Projection Agent detected broken out or busted on the 15m or 1H chart (`strategy.key_levels.patterns`), the way they broke, by the family's rule (`PATTERN_FAMILIES`). The 58 patterns that apply to gold belong to exactly one family; the five event patterns without a gold event belong to none.
  - `peaks` (16: double and triple tops and bottoms, head-and-shoulders, three falling peaks, three rising valleys): the entry on the return to the confirmation line, the stop half the pattern's height behind it (at most 1.25 ATR).
  - `lines` (15: triangles, wedges, rectangles, broadening formations, diamonds): the entry on the return to the broken line, the stop beyond the pattern's other line, where it is busted (at most 1 ATR).
  - `curve` (10: cup with handle, rounding turns, scallops, bump-and-run): the stop a third of the height behind the rim (at most 1.25 ATR); the first target half the measured move, the second the measured target.
  - `bars` (4: horns, pipes): the stop beyond the spikes' own extreme (at most 1.25 ATR).
  - `pole` (3: flags, pennants, the high and tight flag): these seldom return, so the entry is at price within 0.35 ATR of the broken line; the stop beyond the flag's other side (at most 1 ATR).
  - `gaps` (3: gaps, island reversals, long islands): the entry at the gap's edge, the stop beyond its other edge (at most 1 ATR).
  - `legs` (2: measured moves): the entry within 0.35 ATR of the break, the stop beyond the pause between the legs (at most 1.25 ATR).
  - `event` (5: the dead-cat bounce and its inverse, the release surprises, the release flag): the entry within 0.35 ATR of the break, the stop beyond the pattern's other side (at most 1.25 ATR).
  - Common to every family: a "retest" entry is at price within 0.15 ATR of the broken line, else a limit at the line; no plan once price is back inside the pattern or the first target is closer than the mode's gap; the first target is the pattern's measured target (the second half its height beyond) unless said otherwise; 0.1 ATR is added behind every stop. The Projection Agent's own record decides which pattern: one measured below the rate of all patterns (`src/pattern-calibration.mjs`) is left out, the best measured comes first, and the setup names the record. The plan carries its `pattern`.
  - **A break is upward or downward alike** (owner 2026-11-13: "kenapa hanya break out apa break down sudah?"): every agent plans both sides, a long on a pattern broken upward and a short on one broken downward; `breakout_retest` is labelled "Break and retest", and the council's reading says "broke upward" or "broke downward".
  - **Inside the pattern** (`pattern_inside`, owner 2026-11-13 "approve all"): a pattern of the `lines` family that has not broken yet (status forming) and is at least 0.8 ATR wide is traded from one line toward the other: price within the quarter of the pattern next to a line (at most 0.1 ATR beyond it) gives a long from the lower line or a short from the upper line; the entry at price within 0.15 ATR of the line, else a limit 5 % of the width inside it; the stop 0.25 ATR beyond the line; the targets the middle of the pattern and the far line less 10 % of the width. Once a bar closes beyond a line the pattern is broken and belongs to `pattern_lines`.
  - **Record and breaker per pattern:** the plan learner keeps each pattern's own trades (`plan_setups.pattern`; `patterns` in the agent's record). A pattern whose own record with an agent is negative over 15 resolved setups is held back for that agent (`suspendedPatterns`, `PATTERN_BREAKER`; its breaks and its swings inside are judged apart) while the rest of its family trades on; the council's evidence shows `this_pattern_record` beside the family's record; Telegram tells the owner once each way.
- **Status:** `live` (the council may choose it), `shadow` (recorded and measured, never traded, never shown to the council) or `off` (not computed). `zone_pullback` is always live; the others are shadow by default in code. Switches `STRATEGY_AGENTS_LIVE` and `STRATEGY_AGENTS_OFF` (comma-separated ids; off wins) and `STRATEGY_AGENTS_MODE` (`on` by default; `off`: the default agent only). **Production (owner 2026-11-13: "live sekarang langsung implementasi karena strategi lebih related dengan key level bukan decision"): `STRATEGY_AGENTS_LIVE` in `wrangler.jsonc` names `sweep_reversal`, `breakout_retest`, `range_fade`, the eight pattern families and `pattern_inside`: all thirteen agents live.**
- **Breaker** (`suspendedAgents`, `AGENT_BREAKER`): a live agent whose own measured record is negative over at least 30 resolved setups is suspended, back to shadow, where it is still recorded and measured; it is live again as soon as its record is no longer negative. The default agent is never suspended. The Decision Engine reads the records from the plan learner's state at every decision; the ledger step tells the owner once on Telegram when an agent is suspended or live again (`syncAgentBreaker`, KV `strategy-agents:suspended:v1`). The same step keeps the roster as the switches set it (`{ id: { status, version } }` in the same KV value) and sends one `XAUUSD STRATEGY AGENTS` line per agent added, removed, version-bumped or moved between live, shadow and off, and the whole roster once on its first run; a breaker suspension still sends only its own line. Each message is sent before it is stored, and only what was told is kept, so a Telegram failure leaves the stored part unchanged and the next ledger step sends the same lines again. An agent may declare `variants: { key: label }`; the breaker's labels and the consensus name a variant before a catalogue pattern.
- **In the Decision Engine** (`assess`): each side's guardrails carry `plans`, every agent that is not off and sees its setup. The record keeps them as `strategy_plans` (`{ long, short }`: per agent its status, entry, stop, targets, reward and setup). The guardrail's own plan stays the default agent's.
- **The council's choice:** only when a live agent other than the default has a plan on a side that passes the reward guardrail (`agentPlanPasses`: scalp needs a real level as the first target, the other modes the mode's minimum reward) does the evidence list `strategies` on that side (id, name, what it is made for, its setup, its plan and its `record`: setups, mean R per setup, fill and target rates, and the same in the present market condition, trading mode and 1H regime; null while it has no resolved setup), the evidence also carries `price_action`, the Projection Agent's reading of the chart patterns on the 15m, 1H and 4H charts (`priceActionReading`: pattern, status, the way it broke, its record on gold; no price), and the prompt explains both; the answer's optional `strategy` names one. `decide` takes that agent's plan, else the default's; the trade then carries `strategy` and `setup`, and the Telegram message a "Strategy:" line. Where no other live agent sees its setup the evidence, the prompt and the trade are as before. Omega executes an agent's plan like any other: its own stop rules still apply (the 5m structure stop on a market entry, and with `adaptive_volatility` a stop of at least 0.75 × the broker's 15m ATR).
- **A witness of the quorum** (owner 2026-11-13): the live agents' consensus counts in the Decision Engine's quorum with the weight of a timeframe room (`STRATEGY_AGENTS`; "Decision Engine" above). So the agents no longer only offer plans for a side the witnesses chose: the side they see setups on weighs in the choice of side.
- **Execution only per the strategy agents** (owner 2026-11-16, `docs/RFC_STRATEGY_EXECUTION.md`; `STRATEGY_EXECUTION`, `on` by default, `off` is the rollback):
  - **Entry:** a BUY or SELL trades only the plan of an agent that speaks on its side (the consensus' speakers: live, its own setup, its plan passing the reward guardrail, the default agent only at its level). The council's choice is used if it speaks, else the strongest voice. With no speaker, or with the agents' consensus leaning against the stance beyond ±0.15, the stance is held at WAIT (`overruled.by` `strategy_agents`). The trade records its agent (`trade.agent`).
  - **Confidence:** at every 15m decision each live agent gets a confidence per side, `0.40 × direction + 0.35 × setup + 0.25 × quality` (the quorum score signed for the side; speaking here, on neither side, or on the other; its measured voice). The record keeps `agent_confidence`, and `agent_confidence_prev` from the decision exactly one bar before. The signal carries `strategy_execution`, `trade_agent`, `agent_conf` and `agent_conf_prev` (flat `id:long:short` strings).
  - **Exits (Omega):** the lean flip closes every system position against a fresh power beyond ±0.15 (`lean_flip`); a drop of more than 0.02 in the position's agent's confidence closes it when it is in profit (`confidence_drop`; since CH1, 2026-11-23, a drop of at least 0.05 with the change threshold `conf` on); the trailing and extended TP move only while that confidence is stable or rising (with `conf` on an unknown trend reads as the last known one). Manual and external positions are never touched.
- **Measured:** the plan learner follows each agent's own setups (`plan_setups.agent`) and keeps each agent's record (`agents` in its state: setups, mean R, fill, target and stop rates, per context). `GET /api/strategy-agents`: the registry with status, what each is made for and its record, and the latest decision's plans and chosen agent. Nexus shows both on the Strategy agents page (`#/strategy`): the registry, the latest decision's plan per agent, each agent's record per market condition, the plan learner's grid and its referee.

### Plan learner (owner 2026-11-13, `docs/RFC_PLAN_LEARNING.md`, `src/plan-learning.mjs`)
- Goal (owner): "strategy modul learning yang adaptif terhadap price action supaya entry dan resiko lebih presisi". The strategy engine places a plan by fixed rules; this module measures on gold's own price action where the entry and the stop should have been, and learns it per market condition. **Shadow only:** no room, decision, EA signal or Omega order reads it; applying a learned plan needs the owner (BACKLOG item 23).
- **Setup:** one plan the Decision Engine held, the first decision of a run of BUY or SELL decisions with the same side, stop and target (within 0.05 ATR, no gap above 30 minutes). D1 `plan_setups`, one row per setup, with the strategy agent whose plan it is (`agent`): each agent's plan on the decided side from the record's `strategy_plans`, or the trade as `zone_pullback` for a decision of before the agents. The grid below is learned on `zone_pullback`'s setups; every agent's own record stands beside it.
- **Grid:** 20 arms, the entry 0, 0.1, 0.2 or 0.3 × the 1H ATR deeper than the plan's (toward the stop) and the stop 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 1 or 1.25 × the plan's distance from the plan's entry; the target stays the plan's key level; the control `e0@s1` is the plan. An arm that leaves less than 0.25 ATR of risk does not fit a setup.
- **Replay** (`replaySetup`): once the engine stops repeating a plan, each arm on the Signal Ledger's bars (`ledgerSeries`: 1m where the 1m series reaches, 15m before) with the ledger's `simulateSignal` (SL+ at halfway, both in one bar is the stop). A plan at its level is entered at market on the first bar after the decision when that bar opens within 0.35 × the plan's stop distance of the entry, as Omega enters it, and the arm keeps its stop and target distances from that fill; a deeper entry and a pullback plan stay limits (version `cryptophys.plan-learning.v2`; a setup replayed by an older version is replayed again). An entry may fill only while the plan was repeated (at least 15 minutes, at most 8 hours); a filled arm is marked to market after 24 hours. Results in R of the arm's own risk, 0R for no fill; `resolved_at` is the last arm's exit on the bars.
- **Learner** (`learn`): per arm the mean difference against the plan on the same setups; per context (`<trading mode>|<1H regime>`) shrunk toward the estimate over every context and that toward zero (prior 20 setups). A context picks the arm with the largest shrunk gain above 0.05R that was replayed on at least 20 setups, else the plan.
- **Referee** (`walkForward`): each setup's pick from the setups resolved before it opened, compared with the plan on that setup; the test counts only **independent** setups, those that opened after the one counted before them had ended (the engine writes a new plan at almost every 15m close, so setups overlap and share one price move): a one-sided sign test on their older part (p < 0.05, mean difference above zero), their newest quarter (at least 15) held out and required to agree. `collecting` below 60 independent setups or 20 decisive ones, then `better_than_plan` or `not_better`; `all` reports the same comparison over every setup.
- **Run:** in the Signal Ledger's 5-minute step (`runLedgerStep`): the decisions of the last 2 days (30 days at the first run, which replays the stored history), the replay of the ended setups, then the state to KV `plan-learning:state:v1` when something changed.
- **Read:** `GET /api/plan-learning` (the arms' record, the picks per context, the referee, and for the latest decision with a trade the plan and what the learner would place). Switch `PLAN_LEARNING_MODE` (`shadow` by default, `off` records nothing).

### Call for Councils, phase P1: evidence only (owner 2026-11-16: "tahap P1 mulai"; `docs/RFC_CALL_FOR_COUNCILS.md`)
- **What P1 is:** deterministic suggestion flags, an evidence pack per topic, two read-only routes and a Nexus page. No model call, no session, no Durable Object, no kernel report, no Telegram, no write route; nothing a decision, a room, a witness, a plan, the EA signal or Omega reads. Sessions (`CouncilCell`, `council_sessions`, the Omega write route) are phase P2.
- **Topics** (`src/cfc-pack.mjs` `parseTopicKey`, checked strictly against the registry and fixed sets): `strategy:<agent>[:<pattern>|:exits][@<mode>|<regime>]` for any agent of `STRATEGY_AGENTS` and any pattern it trades; `seat:<id>[@<chart>]` (the generated roster without `system`; charts `15`, `60`, `240`, `1D`); `engine:15`, `engine:model`; `risk:<session | trading mode | 1H regime>`; `news:release_horizon | drift | calendar`; `execution:recoveries | rejected_intents | fills | sl_plus`; `data:parity | market_feed | tv_budget | prophet_r37 | prophet_r38 | gmi_v12_70`; `cost:<meter source>` or `cost:shadow`; `knowledge:<learned item id>`; `free:<slug>`.
- **The evidence pack** (`council_pack.v1`: `{ schema, topic, type, as_of, sections, refs, sha256 }`) is built on request from the stored tables, each read bounded and by an index (at most 400 trades of 60 days, 200 shown in a section's statistics and 50 as rows). Strategy: the agent's resolved Decision Engine trades (`signal_ledger` joined to `engine_decisions` `$.trade.agent`, else `$.trade.strategy`, else `zone_pullback`), exits under both SL+ definitions (Omega's stop locked behind passed key levels is the headline, the ledger's breakeven stop beside it) per side, mode, regime, session and entry type, the limit entry at the key level (`ENTER_LIMIT`) apart from the market entry for the topic and for every agent, excursions, the stop distance in 1H ATR, the plan learner's 20 arms on the agent's own `plan_setups`, its record and breaker state, held decisions, the engine's 4-bar outcome on the trades' bars, real fills (`ea_deals`, no money or volume) and the releases inside losing trades. The other types read `outcome_scores` and `scorecard_snapshots`, `compliance_checks`, `engine_decisions`, `auto_tune_runs`, `econ_releases`, `omega_events`, `omega_intents`, `stuck_states`, `health_alerts`, `engine_parity_registry`, `tv_call_budget`, `ai_usage_daily`, `shadow_switch_events`, `learned_live` and `shadow_agent_outputs`. A section whose source does not exist says `unavailable: <reason>` (e.g. the seat's text inside `room_runs`, a news drift alert, owner corrections, earlier council documents). Every price field is dropped (`PRICE_FIELDS`: entry, stop, targets, levels, prices, price distances, money); claims are in R, shares, counts, ATR multiples and times; a model identifier is never kept. Refs are `ev:xauusd/<source>/<id>[@<as_of>][#<field>]` and never name a price field; `sha256` is the hash of the pack's canonical JSON (keys sorted), so the same evidence at the same `as_of` hashes the same. The pack stays behind Access (never to the kernel, Aether or `PublicProjection`).
- **Suggestion flags** (`src/cfc-triggers.mjs`, hourly in the first parity sweep after the hour, skipped while the market is closed): a live agent suspended by its breaker; a pattern held back for an agent; an agent's or a pattern's last 20 resolved trades ending at the stop or SL+ 65 % of the time or more under Omega's policy, the ledger's plan or the plan learner's replay of the agent's own plan (so shadow agents count); an agent's mean R below 0 in one context over 20 setups; a session, mode or regime whose last 20 trades end at the stop or SL+ 65 % or more; the engine's 7-day hit rate 10 points below its 30-day rate (30 decisive calls); the baseline disconnected; a seat at the weight floor (0.25) on a chart; a live note retired as worse; three losing trades in a release window in 7 days; three recoveries or three failed Omega intents in 24 hours; a stuck state over 24 candles, a revoked parity, stale market data; a meter source up 50 % week on week (1,000 neurons a week at least) or a shadow breaker trip. Limits: one open suggestion per topic key (a partial unique index), the same fingerprint (topic key and rule) not again within 7 days, at most 3 new a UTC day. In P1 a suggestion stays open; its signal and `last_seen_at` are refreshed while its flag still fires.
- **Storage:** D1 `cfc_suggestions` (migration `0024`, also made at run time): `id`, `topic_key`, `type`, `rule`, `fingerprint`, `signal_json`, `status` (`open`; `dismissed` arrives with the owner's write route in P2), `created_at`, `last_seen_at`. D1, not KV: the dedup, the cooldown and the daily cap are read-then-write checks KV's eventual consistency could not hold. KV `cfc:model-gate:v1` keeps the baseline gate's last status.
- **Switch:** `CFC_MODE` (`wrangler.jsonc`): `suggest` (the default) runs the hourly flags; `off` runs nothing; `on` is kept for the P2 sessions and in P1 behaves as `suggest`; any other value counts as `off`. No mode starts a session in P1. The evidence route is readable in every mode.
- **Read:** `GET /api/council/suggestions` (the suggestions, open first, the mode, the rules and a catalogue of valid topic keys) and `GET /api/council/evidence?topic=<topic_key>` (400 `invalid_topic` for a key the grammar refuses). Nexus proxies both like every other `GET /api/*`. Nexus shows them on the **Call for Councils** page (`#/councils`), its own item in the left navigator (owner 2026-11-16: "jangan ditampilkan di omega, buat menu navigator sendiri Call for Councils"; nothing of it on the Omega page): the suggested topics as cards, a topic picker (a type, then a key; a strategy agent from `GET /api/strategy-agents` with its pattern, its exits or one market condition) and the pack as tables per section with its refs and sha256. Read-only.

### Indicator manifests and registry (RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P2, owner go 2026-11-07)
- **Manifests** (`src/indicator-manifests.mjs`): one per agent. They cover the engines Prophet R38, Prophet R37 and GMI v12.70, the Mizan library, the 14 HUD agents, the P6 indicator agents, the desks and the moderator. Each manifest gives:
  - kind and name;
  - family;
  - the Pine source and section it reads;
  - the reference: `pine_ssot`, `pine_candidate`, `builtin` or `none`;
  - its parity identity and HUD identity;
  - the rooms it speaks in;
  - its scored subjects, its stuck-state field prefix and its shadow family.
- **Hashed files:** the manifests wrap the existing code without changing any hashed file.
- **Generated from them:** the HUD roster (`HUD_NAMES`, `HUD_IDS`) and the agent families of the crowd line.
- **Registry** (`src/indicator-registry.mjs`, `GET /api/indicator-agents`): every manifest with its live state:
  - **Lifecycle:** `active`, `candidate` (an engine whose H1 identity is not approved) or `not_computed`.
  - **Parity:** every registry identity, per chart.
  - **Scorecards:** 30-day scorecards as an indicator and as a room agent per room.
  - **Stuck states and shadow versions:** the open stuck states and the shadow versions of the agent.
- Read-only. The P4 auditor in the platform can read it.

### New indicator agents: Supertrend and Order Blocks (RFC_INDICATOR_AGENT_PLATFORM P6, 2026-11-07)
- They are the first agents added through the manifest path (owner decision 4: Supertrend first, then Order Block). Both are computed in the HUD snapshot (`kind: "indicator"`), so they speak in every room, D1 to 15m, like a HUD agent.
- **Supertrend** (`local-engines/producer/src/supertrend.mjs`):
  - The TradingView built-in `ta.supertrend(3, 10)`, reproduced on the chart's closed bars: hl2, a Wilder ATR, ratcheting bands, and the direction from the previous line.
  - **Fields:** direction (1 bullish, -1 bearish), line, whether it flipped on this bar, bars since the flip, and the distance in ATR.
  - **Reading:** a pure formula of closed bars, so it reads as "computed from verified bars".
  - **Scoring:** its H1 direction is scored as `indicator:supertrend`. Its line is a key level (0.6 × the chart weight).
- **Order Blocks:**
  - No new definition: it reads the zones the parity-approved Prophet R38 order-block filter already tracks, plus GMI's Aegis validated blocks and mitigation zones.
  - **Fields:** active bullish and bearish blocks; the best block on each side (top and bottom); the next opposite block as a target; the filter status; whether price sits inside a validated block or a mitigation zone.
  - The best blocks' edges are key levels (0.7 × the chart weight).
- **Calibration:** neither has a comparator alert yet (reference `builtin` and `none`), so their readings carry no verified statement. The Supertrend reading counts as computed from verified bars.

### MT5 Expert Advisor (owner decisions 2026-11-09: "Langsung siap live", execution by an EA on the owner's terminal)
- `GET /api/ea/signal` (`src/ea-signal.mjs`, behind Cloudflare Access): one flat object for the EA: `kill` (`EA_SIGNALS_ENABLED = "false"`), `blackout` (the event gate: 2 hours before to 30 minutes after a High-impact USD event), the latest **Decision Engine** decision (since 2026-11-09, section "Decision Engine"; the H1 decision with `DECISION_ENGINE = "off"`) as `side` BUY, SELL or NONE with `action`, `entry`, `stop`, `tp1` (the key-level plan and the chosen target; a plan with a stop or target on the wrong side is never sent), `issued_ms` (the decided bar's close), `expires_ms` (the next close of that timeframe), `source`, and the feed's last 5-minute close (`market_price`) so the EA can turn the levels into distances from its broker's price.
- **Not deployed (owner 2026-11-16):** the owner's VPS runs only the MT5 terminal with OmegaExecutor (`ea/mt5/OmegaExecutor.mq5`), which executes Omega's intents; every entry and exit rule, including `lean_flip` and `confidence_drop` (`docs/RFC_STRATEGY_EXECUTION.md`), runs in Omega. The standalone EA below is kept for reference. It does not have those two exits (BACKLOG B-15).
- The EA (`ea/mt5/CryptophysXAUUSD.mq5`, guide `docs/EA_MT5.md`) runs on the owner's MetaTrader 5 terminal: it sizes from `RiskPercent` of equity and the stop distance, keeps the plan's stop and target distances, and never enters on an old or expired signal, in a blackout, above the spread limit, after the daily loss limit, beyond its position limit, or on a real account without `AllowLiveAccount`. `kill` closes its positions.
- `POST /api/ea/heartbeat` keeps the EA's last state in KV (`ea:heartbeat:last`, 2 days; no account number); `GET /api/ea/status` shows it beside the signal.
- The EA authenticates with a Cloudflare Access service token named `cryptophys-agent-*`, admitted by the platform's **Provision Cloudflare Access** workflow. The server holds no broker credential and gives no position size.

### Omega Execution Engine (owner decisions 2026-11-09: a page separate from Nexus and Aether; monitor + control; its own subdomain; per-position data from the EA)
- **Worker** `xauusd-omega` (`workers/xauusd-omega`), custom domain `omega.cryptophys.work`, no workers.dev or preview URL. A static page (no build) and `/api/overview`, `/api/deals`, `/api/whoami`, `POST /api/control`. Its only data path is the Service Binding `OMEGA` to the AI Worker's RPC entrypoint `OmegaGateway` (`overview`, `deals`, `setControl`).
- **Access:** the hostname is in the platform's Access application (`PUBLIC_HOSTNAMES` of `provision-access.yml`, ADR-0010 amendment of 2026-11-09). The Worker verifies the `Cf-Access-Jwt-Assertion` JWT itself on every request but `/health` (RS256 against `cryptophys.cloudflareaccess.com`, issuer, expiry, and the audience when the deploy could resolve `ACCESS_AUD`), so it fails closed. A control change needs a person (never a service token), a same-origin JSON request, a reason to pause or kill and the typed `KILL`; the page has a strict CSP (same-origin script and style, no inline code).
- **Control** (`src/ea-execution.mjs`, KV `ea:control`): `active`, `paused` (the signal carries `paused`: the EA makes no new entry and deletes its pending orders) or `killed` (the signal's `kill`, `kill_source` `omega`: the EA closes its positions and stops). Each change is audited in D1 `ea_control_events` (time, previous and new mode, reason, the Access email). `EA_SIGNALS_ENABLED = "false"` stays above it (`kill_source` `env`).
- **EA data:** the heartbeat (`POST /api/ea/heartbeat`, 16 KiB) keeps up to 20 open positions (`positions_detail`: ticket, side, volume, entry, stop, target, profit, swap, open time, signal) and the floating and today's P&L; each closed position is `POST /api/ea/deal` into D1 `ea_deals` (once per deal ticket and close time; side, volume, entry and exit, stop and target, profit, commission, swap, reason, signal, open and close times, account mode and currency). No account number anywhere.
- **Statistics** (`dealStats`, net = profit + commission + swap): trades, wins, win rate, net, profit factor, average win and loss, maximum drawdown; today (UTC), 7 and 30 days.
- v1 (above) never places an order. The broker engine below does, through the owner's own MT5 terminal. Omega is unrelated to the legacy `omega` execution swarm of `cryptophys-cloud-platform`, which stays prohibited and was never migrated.

### Omega broker engine (owner decisions 2026-11-09: "Omega memegang akses broker"; "halaman omega masih di nexus.cryptophys.work/omega; di halaman ini bisa input server, user, dan password untuk terhubung broker yang dipilih"; "Bridge di host ini"; "Mati sampai Anda aktifkan")
- **Who does what:** Omega decides and sizes (`src/omega-engine.mjs`, pure); the AI Worker keeps the state and runs the plan on each bridge sync (`src/omega-broker.mjs`, D1 `omega_settings`, `omega_connection`, `omega_bridge`, `omega_intents`, `omega_events`); the **Omega bridge** on the owner's MT5 host (`bridge/omega-bridge`, a Node daemon, plus `ea/mt5/OmegaExecutor.mq5` in the terminal) logs the terminal in and executes. Contract: `docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE_PROTOCOL.md`; install: `docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE.md`. **Sync cost (owner 2026-11-14, "B. penuh tanpa fase shadow"):** the `omega_bridge` row is written when something that matters changes (key, version, connection, trading permission, terminal error, executor, account mode or currency, the open positions' and orders' tickets, volumes and stops, the deal tickets; `bridgeStateKey`) and otherwise every 30 seconds (`BRIDGE_WRITE_EVERY_MS`), so the page and the health check show the last quote and sync time at most 30 seconds late; the planner plans on the sync's own state. A reported deal is written once per isolate. The EA control (pause, kill) is read once each sync and never cached, on every full plan.
  - **Limit entries, stage 1 (2026-11-20, owner; docs/RFC_OMEGA_LIMIT_ENTRY.md).** Setting `entry_order` ("market" default; "limit" refused until stage 3, `LIMIT_ENTRY_AVAILABLE`). Omega's own pending limits in the terminal's report (`omegaPendingOrders`: `BUY_LIMIT`/`SELL_LIMIT` with its magic or source) count as positions (`max_positions`, `same_side_unprotected`; since SS1, 2026-11-23, not one of an older signal on the current signal's side, which is deleted as `superseded_by_signal`, below) and as risk at their stops (`pendingRiskAtStops`, in the daily budget), and are deleted at once without the council when a hard reason holds (`pendingDeleteReason`: the switch on market, disarm on a real account (`disarmed`, owner or auto-disarm; ADR-0020 Amendment 7), pause, blackout, symbol, tick value or money failing, an unvalidated or downgrading profile, the daily loss floor; `engine_off` and kill too). An Omega pending order without a stop is deleted too (`pending_without_stop`, said as a note): Omega never places one. With `entry_order` "market" every such order is deleted (`entry_order_market`), so none survives stage 1. Deletes are `delete_order` intents, one per order and minute (`del:<ticket>:<minute>`). OmegaExecutor 1.21 adds `place_limit` and `delete_order`, its own expiry timer (never GTC) and a dead-man switch (docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE.md); since 1.22 (2026-11-22) its own deletions (`ea:del:<ticket>`) carry their reason (`expired`, `max_life`, `dead_man`), which the event and the owner's line show (`eaDeletedLine`).
  - **Limit entries, stage 3: live (2026-11-20, owner; docs/RFC_OMEGA_LIMIT_ENTRY.md §2-§6).** `LIMIT_ENTRY_AVAILABLE` is true. `entry_order` is `"market"` (default, today's path, byte for byte: test S12 hashes 64 hand fixtures and v4's replay traces taken before), `"limit"` or `"council"`, the owner's switch on the Omega page; a switch to limit or council needs OmegaExecutor 1.21 in the terminal's last state (`ea_1_21_required`, also refused by planIntents if an older EA reports later). Aether's policy never writes it.
    - **Placement (`limitPlacement`).** After every gate of today, in order, then `pending_order_exists` (one Omega pending order at a time; an order SS1 supersedes is not counted, its delete goes first), `platform_cap` (positions plus pending orders at 3 per account, ADR-0020 A7) and `ea_1_21_required`: the limit is `signal.entry + feed_basis` (`basis_unknown` without one), a buy below the ask and a sell above the bid (`limit_through_price`), at least the stop level away (`limit_stops_level`), at most `placement_band` × the plan's stop (`limit_out_of_band`), never on a netting account (`netting_account`). In limit mode a refusal is a missed trade, never a market entry. The stop and target keep their distances from the limit (the adaptive stop included); the lot is today's sizing at the limit price with every hard guard. Expiry: placement + `max_pending_life` (`limitKnobs`, `src/omega-limit-knobs.mjs`, shared with the ledger's limit policy), never GTC.
    - **Intents (EA 1.21, the bridge unchanged).** `place_limit` (id = the signal's open id, `volume` `<lot>@<limit>`, `ticket` the expiry in UTC epoch seconds; its payload keeps the placing decision's quorum share, its close, the profile, the knobs and the lot), `modify_order` (the new price in `volume`), `delete_order`.
    - **The entry council (`src/limit-council.mjs`, AI task and meter `entry_council`).** The sync files the signal (`entry_request`, D1 `limit_council`); `runLimitCouncil` in the 5-minute ledger step, after the Position Manager, answers it in one model call (Omega's entry seat and the PM's seat; the 15m decision with its reasons, the agent's confidence trend, the quorum share, the 1H, 4H and 1D rooms' latest readings and biases); the next sync places. Limit: place or skip; skip or no answer, no order (since CH1, 2026-11-23, with the change threshold `skip` on, a skip needs a measured skip reason that holds, else the limit is placed, `skip_no_measured_reason`; `confidence_drop` and `quorum_share_drop` hold by the `take_profit_weakening` thresholds of the mode). Council: `enter_market`, `place_limit` or `skip`, each kept only with a measured reason that holds (`htf_against`, `confidence_drop`, `quorum_share_drop`; `price_away_from_level`; `price_at_level`, `htf_with`, `confidence_rising`), else the market entry; silent (a failure, the cap, or no answer 6 minutes after the decision, `ENTRY_COUNCIL_WAIT_MS`) is today's market path. Its spend shares the Position Manager's daily cap (`POSITION_COUNCIL_DAILY_USD`).
    - **While pending.** Deterministic cancels at every sync (`pendingDeleteReason`, after stage 1's reasons): `daily_risk_budget`, `pending_unrecorded`, `profile_downgrade`, `de_veto` (the decision's veto of the side, `veto_long`/`veto_short` on the signal), `quorum_share_drop` (the side's share, `quorum_long_share`/`quorum_short_share`, fell by more than `cancel_share_drop` since the placing decision; since CH1 with the token `limit` on, by at least it, scalp 0.10, intraday and range 0.10, swing 0.15, outside scalp confirmed by the decision before), `pending_expired`. At each 15m close the council keeps, moves (to a broker key level toward price, at most `move_max_atr` × ATR(14) of the feed's 15m bars, the stop and target shifted by the same amount, the lot untouched) or cancels; council mode may also convert once to market (the delete first, `del:<ticket>:cv`; the market leg `<open id>:m` only after the delete is answered and the order is gone, never after a fill, sized afresh through today's gates). A move or cancel needs a measured reason (the decision against the side, since CH1 on 2 consecutive closed decisions; the agent's confidence trend a drop; price more than `move_max_atr` × ATR from the order), else `keep_no_reason`; a silent council cancels at that close (`council_unavailable`). The sync re-checks each action against the live price (`limitActionIntents`).
    - **A newer same-side signal supersedes a resting limit (SS1, owner 2026-11-23, relayed by the orchestrator: "Ya, sinyal baru menggantikan (Rekomendasi)"; `src/limit-supersede.mjs`).** Behind the Omega setting `limit_supersede`, `"on"` (default) or `"off"`, the owner's switch only (validated in `settingsFrom`; Aether's policy and its rollback never write it; labelled in the change audit; in `ERA_SWITCHES`, `limit_supersede=off` starts a new era; no environment variable). `"off"` is today's engine byte for byte (test SS1-9 against main's code). The case: at 14:10:54Z the 14:00 BUY (breakout_retest) rested a BUY limit at its zone's edge (`open:1795442400000:B`, 30 minutes) that never filled; at 14:17:23Z the 14:15 BUY (zone_pullback, price inside its new zone) was skipped `same_side_unprotected` because the resting limit counted; price then ran up.
      - **Rule.** Under `entry_order` limit or council, after the deterministic cancels above: an Omega pending limit kept by them, on the side of the current signal, whose placing signal is older (its `place_limit` payload's `signal_issued_ms`, else the order comment `open:<issued>:<B|S>`; unknown: never) is deleted, `delete_order` reason `superseded_by_signal` (`del:<ticket>:<minute>`, with `side`, `signal_issued_ms` and `superseded_by`). It then counts in none of the new signal's gates (`same_side_unprotected`, `max_positions`, `platform_cap`, `pending_order_exists`, the daily budget's pending risk); its risk still counts in the fill guard and A8's budget until it is gone, and a council action on it is dropped. The new signal goes through every gate in the same plan (the entry council's request or answer, the band, EZ1/EZ1b, `zone_*`, the caps).
      - **Unchanged.** Open positions (a filled limit included): `same_side_unprotected` as before. A resting limit on the other side, or of the same signal: `pending_order_exists` / `already_opened` as before. `entry_order` market deletes every Omega limit as before (`entry_order_market`).
      - **Refused.** The new signal refused (council skip or silence, out of band, expired, …): the old limit is cancelled all the same. The plan's note `superseded_by_signal` names the ticket and both signals and ends with the new signal's outcome ("the new signal: council_skip").
      - **Delete first, then place.** While a superseded order is still in the terminal's report (its delete not done, or refused and sent again a minute later), or a `superseded_by_signal` delete of the last 10 minutes is still unanswered (`omega_intents` pending or sent; `limitInputs` reads them only with the switch on; a failed read holds), no entry is planned in that sync (note `superseded_delete_waiting`); the next sync places it once the delete is done. Telegram: the cancellation's line with the reason `superseded_by_signal`, then the placement's.
      - **Ledgers.** The limit counterfactual beside v4 (`src/ledger-omega-limit.mjs`) ends a resting order of an older signal at the minute's open when the current signal is on its side (missed, gate `superseded_by_signal`) and places the new one in that minute on planIntents' gates (live, the place follows at the next sync, seconds later). v4 is the market replay, with no resting order: its records are unchanged. The v3 Signal Ledger's Omega entry (`omegaEntry`) ends by the next 15m close, so an older row never rests at a newer decision: `blocksNewDecision` (LH1) releases it (skipped) or holds it as an open position (filled, unprotected); only a row not simulated yet keeps LH1's hold inside its window. No change there.
      - **Hashes.** `ENGINE_BEHAVIOUR_HASH` 39c10287… → ecfe3f90… (a recorded exception, a new parity era at the deploy: the fingerprint's group `ss/`, planIntents under limit and council and the limit counterfactual, written with the switch on); with `limit_supersede` "off" every fixture still gives 39c10287… byte for byte. `ENGINE_CODE_HASH` 38aa8e6f… → 7b23836a….
    - **The fill (`fillGuard`).** The position whose ticket is the order's: `tick_value_mismatch`, `volume_below_min`/`volume_above_max`, the profile's hard cap and total open risk, the daily risk budget with this position; a breach closes it at once (`fill_guard_<name>`). The fill is recorded once on the place_limit intent (time, price, `fill_gap_r` in R) and as the event `limit_filled` (R and times only); the Position Manager, the ladder and the agents read it as any Omega open (their queries take `place_limit` with `open`). The v4 parity leaves limit fills out (v4 is the market replay and forces `entry_order: "market"`).
    - **Telegram.** Placed, moved, cancelled (with the reason, the EA's own deletions included) and filled, through `sendOmegaTelegram` to `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID`, the owner's private chat; never a public or CTA channel.
    - **Nexus.** The Omega page's Engine panel has the Entry order selector (limit and council disabled with the reason below EA 1.21); the Pending Orders table shows each Omega limit's status, last council reason and expiry, with its recent events (`GET /omega/api/overview` `entry_order`).
  - **Calm mode, since 2026-11-19** (owner: "Keduanya, bertahap", step 2; omega-bridge 1.7.0, `bridgeStreamMode`; `docs/OMEGA_BRIDGE.md`). Each sync response carries `stream_mode`. It is `calm` while the terminal is connected with no position, no pending order, no open intent, no recovery and no review; then a 1.7.0 bridge syncs every `OMEGA_SYNC_MS` (5 s) and on results, not on each state.json write. Anything else is `fast`, and the bridge goes fast by itself when its own state shows a position, an order or a lost connection. An entry planned while calm reaches the terminal at most 5 s later. Switches: `OMEGA_BRIDGE_CALM` "off" (Worker) and `OMEGA_CALM_MODE=false` (bridge).
  - **Light sync, since 2026-11-19** (owner: "Keduanya, bertahap", step 1; `lightSyncEligible`). The bridge syncs on every state.json write, about 1.7 times a second, and a full plan costs about 15 D1 statements. A sync reuses this isolate's last full plan and only delivers the open intents (about 3 statements) when all of these hold:
    - that plan is under `LIGHT_SYNC_MAX_AGE_MS` (5 s) old;
    - nothing that matters changed (`bridgeStateKey`, the connection, the key, the bridge version);
    - the terminal is connected with no open position and no pending order;
    - the request carries no result and no corpus, macro or calendar data.
  - **What it costs.** A new signal, a settings change or a kill with nothing open is planned at most 5 s later. Any open position always gets the full plan.
  - **Step 2** is a bridge version that slows its own stream when the server says so; it needs the owner's VPS update.
- **Credentials:** the page takes the broker server, login and password and encrypts them in the browser (`workers/xauusd-omega/public/omega-crypto.js`: AES-256-GCM, the key wrapped with RSA-OAEP SHA-256 to the bridge's RSA-3072 public key). D1 keeps only the envelope, the server name and a version; no Worker can read the login or the password, and none is ever returned to a browser. A new bridge key voids the stored envelope (`needs_reentry`). Disconnect bumps the version and the bridge clears the terminal login.
- **The bridge** reaches `POST https://nexus.cryptophys.work/omega/api/bridge/sync` with one Cloudflare Access service token whose client id is the Omega Worker var `OMEGA_BRIDGE_CLIENT_ID` (empty: every bridge is refused). It only makes outbound requests. Its executor state never carries a login.
- **Switches:** the engine plans nothing unless `OMEGA_ENGINE_ENABLED = "true"` (AI Worker var, `"false"` in this repository) **and** the page's Engine toggle is on. A real (or contest) account needs the typed `LIVE` arm; turning the engine off disarms it. A kill (`EA_SIGNALS_ENABLED = "false"` or the Omega control `killed`) plans one `close_all`, also with the engine off; `paused` stops new entries.
- **The plan** on each sync, from the EA signal (`eaSignal`: the Decision Engine's 15m decision, or the H1 decision with `DECISION_ENGINE = "off"`), the control, the settings and the executor state: nothing when the executor is missing, disconnected or older than 60 s; close a position against the signal; open at most once per signal (`open:<issued_ms>:<B|S>`) only before `expires_ms`, within `max_signal_age_ms` (10 min, like the EA), outside the event blackout, at or below `max_spread_points` (60), below `max_positions` (1) and above the daily loss limit (`max_daily_loss_pct` 2 % of the day's starting balance; deposits and withdrawals are never the day's loss or profit, owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju keduanya": the broker's day start, as the EA reports it, is the balance less today's closed deals, so it already holds them and is used as it is; an onboarding reference already moves with them; only the owner's reset over the day's stop is moved by the operations after it (`dailyLossExecutor`, D1 `omega_accounts.day_ops`). Fixed 2026-11-19: the broker's start was moved too, so a $300 deposit counted twice and read as a daily loss). **The connected account** is the one whose opaque reference (`account_ref`, from the EA's login hash) the last bridge sync stored with the state (`connectedAccount`), never the first account on the same server: the 5-minute profile agent, the mode selector, the owner's controls and the overview's `connected` and `day_pl_pct` read it (fixed 2026-11-19, when a USD and a cent account on one server were mixed up). Every owner control (profile, ceiling, agent mode, accept, decline, reset) acts on the connected account only and refuses any other, naming it (each refusal is recorded as the event `profile_control_refused`); each account carries `identity` (USD or cent, currency, gold symbol) and the Console shows it (owner 2026-11-19: "harus bisa membedakan mana akun sen dan usd"). **Daily risk budget and no stacking** (owner 2026-11-12, after four SELL stops lost 4.06 % of the day on 2026-11-12 against the 2 % limit): the day can still lose the equity above the floor (the day's starting balance less `max_daily_loss_pct`) minus what the open positions would lose from the broker's price to their stops (`openRiskAtStops`; the equity carries their floating profit and loss, so a protected position adds only the floating profit it could give back; a position without a stop counts as the whole account; manual positions count, other symbols are not valued); a new entry's lot is capped to that budget at its stop (note `lot_capped_daily_budget`), and below the minimum lot there is no entry (`daily_risk_budget`). A second position on a side opens only once every position already on that side is protected, its stop at or beyond its open price (`isProtected`; note `same_side_unprotected`). A resting Omega limit counts as such a position, except one of an older signal on the current signal's side under `entry_order` limit or council (SS1, `limit_supersede` on: deleted as `superseded_by_signal`); an open position, a filled limit included, always counts. SL and TP keep the plan's distances from the broker's own price (BUY from the ask, SELL from the bid). Volume = `risk_pct` (0.5 %, at most 2 %) of equity / the loss per lot at the stop, floored to the volume step; below the minimum there is no trade. Every skip is a note code; a change of the notes is one `plan` event. Further gates and management (reconciled with the code 2026-11-11, `src/omega-engine.mjs`): no new entry in the daily rollover quarantine (21:55 to 22:15 UTC, `ROLLOVER_QUARANTINE_UTC`); with `adaptive_volatility` (on by default) a stop closer than 0.75 x the symbol's ATR is widened to it, the volume is sized on the widened stop and the target moves in proportion, so the trade keeps the plan's reward-to-risk (audit fix 2026-11-11; note `adaptive_stop`); a limit entry waits while price is more than 0.35 stops away toward the target (`waiting_for_pullback`), and since 2026-11-13 (owner: "setuju") also while it is more than 0.35 × the plan's stop past the entry toward the stop (`price_past_entry`): before, such an entry kept the plan's stop distance from its fill and so put the stop well beyond the plan's key level (a SELL at 3903.98 against a 3888.48 entry); positions are closed on an `EXIT`/`REVERSAL`/mean-reversal signal. **Position management** (`manageIntents`, audit fix 2026-11-11) runs on every sync with the engine on and a fresh executor, whatever the entry gates say (blackout, pause, spread, rollover, full book, no side): with `trailing_stop_r` a position at that many R from its open price moves its stop to the open price (`break_even`); a farther TP2 or extended target of the current signal moves the take-profit of the signal's side; a position of 0.02 lots or more banks half at 1.5R or its target, once per position (`tp1_scale_out`). Before, management ran only when a new entry passed every gate, break-even read a field the executor never sends (`entry` instead of `open_price`) and fired on any profit, and a scaled position could be halved again.
- **Range mode step 1: the H4/D1 trend filter and the evidence gate per cell** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; `src/htf-cells.mjs`). The HTF trend is H4 and D1 each "up", "down" or "flat" (EMA(50) and its slope over 5 closed bars), combined ("mixed" when they disagree); a side is "with", "against" or "neutral" to it. The range strategies (`RANGE_GATED_AGENTS`: `range_fade`, `sweep_reversal`) trade only with the trend and only in a cell (agent × side × HTF context) whose replayed record (the plan learner's control arm, `plan_setups.htf`, state `cells`) has 30+ setups at +0.10R or better (`CELL_GATE`); otherwise the signal has no side and the Omega page says why (`htf_against`, `htf_unknown`, `cell_collecting`, `cell_below_bar`). Applied in `eaSignal` before the strategy arm, so every sizing and risk check stays in force. `RANGE_CELL_GATE` "off" stops it (emergency only).
- **Range, the 4th trading mode** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; `TRADING_MODES_LIST`). Only the range strategies trade in it, through the HTF filter and the cell gate, and only while the decision's 1H regime ranges (`applyRangeMode` in `eaSignal`: `range_mode_agent`, `range_breakout`); intraday thresholds, intraday ladder. The selector proposes it while ranging when a range cell (with the trend or neutral) passes (`rangeEvidence`), through the mode council; the owner may pin it. `rangeExitTick` (5-minute ledger step) returns to the mode it came from (`range_return`) once the 1H regime trends, without the council.
- **The selector's daily outcome** (MS1, 2026-11-23; `runModeSelector`, once a UTC day from the ledger cron's `modeSelectorTick`). Every outcome is one Omega event (`omega_events`, actor `agent:mode`, action `mode_selector_run`, classified `NOT_A_CHANGE` in the change audit; a switch keeps its own `mode_changed` row and event): `{ day, status, reason, action, mode, to, proposed, regime, evidence }` with `status` `pinned_by_server` (reason `server_trading_mode`), `pinned` (`owner_pin` or `auto_off`), `unchanged` (the step's `none` with its reason, e.g. `current_mode_best`, `candidate_holding`, `evidence_collecting`; or `council_rejected`), `changed` or `failed` (the error); `evidence` per mode `{ n, mean_r, status, scope }` (no stop, no price), read on a pinned day too; `fresh` (already run today) and `unavailable` record nothing. Best effort: a failed write never changes the outcome. The setups load by `opened_at`, then `setup_key`, so the walk-forward's holdout (its last 30 %) is the same on every run.
- **Counter-trend reversal probation** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; `src/htf-cells.mjs` `PROBATION`). The reversal strategy (`sweep_reversal`) against the H4/D1 trend, before its cell passes: only after a change of structure on a closed H1 or H4 candle (`structureShift`), at half the risk and half the hard cap (`signal.probation`, `risk_scale` in `planIntents`), one at a time (`probation_busy`), never hedged (`probation_hedge`). It graduates to full risk when its cell passes and reverts when its last 5 probation trades average under 0R (`probationTick` in the 5-minute ledger step, KV `omega:probation:v1`). The owner's switch: `POST /omega/api/probation` (on clears reverted cells).
- **Modify and Delegate on the Trade tab** (owner 2026-11-19: "di tab trade perlu ditambah modify untuk manual interuption Entry/TP/SL dan juga delegate supaya entry manual selanjutnya dihandle oleh omega"; confirmed: "Posisi + pending order", "Omega lanjut biasa", "Tombol + toggle", "Kelola penuh"; `src/omega-hitl.mjs`, OmegaExecutor 1.18). **Modify:** the owner sets an open position's S/L and T/P (Omega's or manual) and a pending limit or stop order's price, S/L and T/P from the Trade tab; `POST /omega/api/position/modify` (owner only, same origin) reaches `ownerModify`, which checks them against the live price and the broker's stop distance (a BUY's stop below the bid, its target above; a SELL the other way; a BUY LIMIT below the ask, and so on; empty removes a level), queues one `modify_sltp` or `modify_order` intent with a `manual:` id and audits `owner_modify_position` / `owner_modify_order` with the values before and after. Omega keeps managing an Omega position afterwards: a later ladder lock or trail may move the stop again. **Delegate:** "DELEGATE" on a manual position (`POST /omega/api/position/delegate`), or the toggle "Delegate my next manual entries to Omega" (`POST /omega/api/delegate-mode`): every manual position opened while it is on is delegated at the next sync (event `position_delegated` with `auto`); "TAKE BACK" releases one, and a released position is never adopted again by the toggle. D1 `omega_delegations` and `omega_delegate_mode`. A delegated position is managed like Omega's own (`isOmegaOwned`): a stop from the plan when it has none (the signal's stop distance on its side, else 1.5 × ATR(M15), from its open price; `delegatedStopIntents`), break-even, the TP ladder and lock (its mode is the current one, R from its stop), the Position Manager (its own stop and target as its plan), the closes on an opposite signal; never an add, a larger size or a reversal. Its intents carry a `d:` id (`markDelegatedIntents`). The overview carries `delegation` (tickets, toggle, `supported`); the page marks a delegated position "MANUAL → OMEGA". Both need OmegaExecutor 1.18: before it Nexus refuses an edit (`ea_1_18_required`) and delegated positions wait (note `delegate_needs_ea_1_18`).
- **Universal broker, server and symbol** (owner 2026-11-19: "desain tolol kalau hardcoded ke broker/server tertentu seharusnya berlaku universal"; OmegaExecutor 1.19, omega-bridge 1.5.0). Nothing in Omega depends on a broker, server or symbol name. **Gold** is what the terminal reports: profit currency USD, a contract of 1 to 100 oz, no `symbol_error`, and a bid within 2% of the reference (`goldSymbolCheck`, `goldPriceProblem` against the signal's entry, note `symbol_price_mismatch`; the EA checks the same against `omega/reference.txt` from the sync's `reference_price`). The owner's symbol is a hint tried first under the same checks. Base currency is not used (FxPro `GOLD` reports base `USD`). **Account money** (`accountMoney` in `src/gold-symbol.mjs`, `toUsd` in `src/omega-broker.mjs`): units of the account currency per US dollar are 1 for `USD`; otherwise measured from the account's own positions and deals (profit ÷ (move × volume × contract), moves of 50+ ticks, median of the latest 8); otherwise the owner's value given with the connection (`units_per_usd`, D1 `omega_connection.units_per_usd`, Setup → Connection & EA "Units per US dollar"); otherwise nothing opens (`money_unverified`), and a measurement more than 5% from the owner's value opens nothing (`money_mismatch`). Every amount is divided once by that number; `cent` means about 100, never a currency code. The tick-value check (`tick_value_mismatch`) and `volume_below_min` stay. Deals stored before #461 keep the USC/USX rule (`isLegacyCentDeal`). **Labels:** the broker's name is the EA's `account.company`, otherwise the full server name. The Console has no broker presets: the server is typed as MT5 shows it. Arming, resets and profile changes stay the owner's.
- **Symbol identity, money and the account card (follow-up, owner 2026-11-19 relayed: "harus diperbaiki semua sampai ultimate, dan ini yang kubilang tolol, kosmetic hardcoded ke broker/server/account tertentu"; OmegaExecutor 1.20, omega-bridge 1.6.0).** **Identity:** the sync's `reference` (`referenceFrom` in `src/gold-symbol.mjs`) carries the feed's last 5-minute close with its time and the last 12 closed 4h closes of `OANDA:XAUUSD`; none while the feed runs on the broker's bars (`fallbackSinceMs`). EA 1.20 chooses a symbol only when its own M5 closes follow those closes (2% level, 0.5% path), refuses contracts with an expiry, refuses to pick between passing symbols with different contracts unless the owner names one, re-checks the chosen symbol against every fresh reference and ages the reference on the price's own time. **Money:** a measurement that agrees with the owner's value converts with the owner's value (`source: "owner_verified"`); without one, a measurement within 1% of a power of ten is that power (`snapPowerOfTen`). The state D1 keeps (`omega_bridge.state`) is the converted executor: read back with `executorFrom(state, { stored: true })` it is never converted again, and the live push converts the raw state with the connection's value. **Blocks:** a daily-loss block records the raw inputs it stands on (`upgrade_block_evidence`, `blockEvidence`: the EA's day start, balance, equity, the limit, and the row's onboarding, day operations and override); each agent tick runs the current day-reference code (`dailyLossExecutor`) on those recorded inputs and lifts the block only when it finds no loss there (event `upgrade_block_lifted`). Only a code change can lift a block; the market, the broker's day rolling before the UTC day, a withdrawal or the clock never do. A block without evidence runs to its end. The EA keeps its verified symbol across restarts (`omega/verified_symbol.txt`, this login only) and skips gaps in the price path (6 matched closes over 12 hours, 75-minute windows); the server leaves out the 4h bars inside the last recorded MT5 fallback, and the Console names ambiguous symbols with their contracts (`eaRefusalText`). **Account card and texts:** the Risk profile card shows the connected account only, described by what the terminal reports (currency, gold symbol, the broker's own name `account.company`); no list of other accounts, no internal id, no server name, in the card, the confirm, the history line, Telegram (`Omega · USD account · GOLD: ...`), refusals and the pages' broker label (`broker`: the company, else "MT5 broker"). The internal id travels in the requests only, so the server refuses anything but the connected account.
- **Cent accounts and the owner's gold symbol** (owner 2026-11-18, "Setuju"; OmegaExecutor 1.17, omega-bridge 1.4.0). **Symbol:** the owner may set the account's gold symbol with the connection (Setup → Connection & EA, the "Symbol" selector, "Other" for a typed name such as `GoldOne`; `trade_symbol`, D1 `omega_connection.trade_symbol`, cleared with the connection). It is the one extra name the server (`goldCheckOf` in `src/gold-symbol.mjs`), the bridge (`stateSymbolProblem`) and the EA (`omega/trade_symbol.txt`) accept, under the same contract checks; the bridge opens its chart first. A name off the list also opens nothing until its bid is within 2% of the signal's reference entry (`mappedPriceProblem`, note `symbol_price_mismatch`). **USC and USX (US cents):** `executorFrom` divides every amount once by 100 (`centToUsd`: balance, equity, the day's start and result, free margin, a lot's margin, each position's and deal's profit, commission and swap). **Tick value** (incident 2026-11-18: GoldOne on a USX account reported 0.01, its value in USD, and a 3-lot SELL risked ~82% of $54.50): for a USD-profit symbol on a USD or cent account Omega sizes on contract size × tick size dollars (`usdTickValue`), keeps the broker's figure as `tick_value_reported`, and opens nothing when that figure is neither the computed value nor 100 times it (`tick_value_mismatch`); other accounts keep the broker's figure, so limits, sizing, baselines and pages are in USD; the account keeps `currency: "USC"` with `cent: true`, the pages label it CENT, and its deals are stored in USD. A USC profit currency passes only on a USC account. **Terminal errors:** while no EA state exists, the bridge sends the terminal journal's last error (`bridge_symbol.terminal_error`; a run of 5+ digits outside a price becomes `[account]`, on the host and again on the server, `redactAccount`); the server records a `terminal_error` event once per change and the overview shows it for 30 minutes after the connection was stored.
- **5m execution** (owner 2026-11-12, `docs/RFC_OMEGA_5M_EXECUTION.md`; with the broker fields on the signal, `OMEGA_EXEC_5M` on). **The stop** of a market entry is the swing low (BUY) or high (SELL) of the last 3 broker 5m bars beyond a buffer of max(2 spreads, 0.1 × the 5m ATR), used only when it is tighter than the plan's stop and no closer than max(0.5 × the 5m ATR, the stops level + 3 spreads); the target stays the plan's (the RRR rises); the adaptive volatility stop is skipped for it (note `stop_5m`). The lot is sized on it but capped at `tight_stop_max_lot_factor` (1 to 2, default 1.5) × the lot at the plan's stop (note `lot_capped_5m`). A limit keeps the plan's stop. The open intent records `stop_source` (`stop_5m`/`plan`) and `trigger_source`. **Management** of Omega's own positions: from 1R (or once at break-even and in profit) the stop trails the 3-bar 5m swing minus the buffer, only forwards and at most once per broker 5m bar (`ms:<ticket>:t5:<minute>`, reason `trail_5m`); from 0.5R in profit (or protected) a broker 5m Prophet confirmation against the position closes it (`cl:<ticket>:o5:<minute>`, reason `opposite_5m`). Break-even and the TP1 scale-out are unchanged. **Key-level management** (owner 2026-11-12: "semua dikontrol dari omega bukan EA", "atur SL+ dan Trailing TP juga harus berdasarkan keylevel menyesuaikan Bull/Bear Power"; Power = the Decision Engine's quorum score): the decision record keeps the Key Level Agent's 12 candidate zones nearest to price (`key_levels`), and the signal carries, whatever the stance, `power` (the latest quorum score, -1 to +1) and `power_ms`, `key_levels` ("low:high" on the feed's scale), `trading_mode` and `feed_basis` (the broker's M1 close minus the feed's 5m close, the median over the last two hours, at least 3 matches, kept 5 minutes per isolate; `feed_basis_known`). With a known basis Omega moves the levels onto the broker's scale and, per own position (`keyLevelPlan`, power signed for the position, fresh for 35 minutes): **SL+** once price passed a key level wholly beyond the entry, the stop locks break-even plus max(2 spreads, the buffer) and steps behind the passed levels: strong power (≥ 0.5) behind the one before the last (room to run), normal behind the last, weak (< 0.2) at the tighter of that and the broker's 5m swing (reason `sl_key_level_<band>`, id `ms:<ticket>:k:<cents>`); **trailing TP** near the target (within max(0.5 × 5m ATR, 3 spreads, 0.2 × the open-to-target distance)) with a strong power the target moves to the next key level (`trail_tp_key_level`) and the stop to the old target less the buffer when it leaves the stop distance; with a weaker power the target is taken; **close** a position in profit when the power is -0.5 or worse against it (`power_reversal`, `cl:<ticket>:pw:<minute>`). The stop only moves forwards and keeps the stops level plus a spread from price. These replace the 5m trail and the extended target, which remain the fallback without a known basis; the 5m opposite exit and the scale-out stay. In scalp an adaptive stop no longer moves the target: it stays at the key level. OmegaExecutor 1.11 turns its local break-even and micro trailing off by default (`AutoBreakEvenEnabled`, `TrailingStopEnabled`): before, they moved stops on the terminal beside Omega (2026-11-11: two shorts' stops moved to about 3990 by the EA, gapped out at the 23:00 reopen). **OmegaExecutor 1.12** (owner 2026-11-12: "ya tambahkan versi EA dan status input ke server") writes `executor` into `state.json`: its name, version and the inputs `AutoBreakEvenEnabled`, `TrailingStopEnabled`, `DynamicAtrStopEnabled`. The bridge passes it through; the AI Worker keeps it checked (`executorInfoFrom`: a malformed value is null) and the overview's `bridge.executor` reads `ok` (1.12 or newer, local break-even and trailing off), `local_management` (one of them still on: the EA moves stops beside Omega), `outdated` (older than `EXECUTOR_MIN_VERSION`) or `unknown` (1.11 and older report nothing), with a line both Omega pages show. While the EA still trails by itself the plan carries the note `ea_local_management`; it never blocks a trade.
- **Same-side re-entry guard** (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"; `reentryGate` in `src/omega-engine.mjs`, the latest exit from `lastWeakeningExit` in `src/omega-broker.mjs`). After a Position Manager close for weakening (`pm_take_profit_weakening`) or a cut loss (`pm_cut_loss`) in the last 6 hours, a new entry on the same side waits only until the next M5 candle closes after the exit (`reentry_cooldown_bars`, 1 closed M5 candle, 0 to 12; note `reentry_cooldown` with the time it frees). Owner 2026-11-18 (confirmed: "Setuju keduanya"): the 0.5×ATR pullback and the confidence bypass were removed. Exits by TP, the ladder or the stop are not weakening exits; the other side is never blocked. The 1m re-entry after a profit-lock stop (RE1, above) is apart from it: it is never offered after these exits, and the cooldown applies to a re-entry too.
- **TP ladder** (owner 2026-11-18, relayed and confirmed: "a, langsung live, termasuk agent juga bisa dinamis mengatur TP sebagian secara progresive"; every mode, Scalp included; `ladderPlan` in `src/omega-engine.mjs`, setting `tp_ladder`, on by default). It replaces the single TP1 scale-out of Omega's own positions; the other management above is unchanged.
  - **Rungs.** TP1..TPn are the near edges of the key levels wholly between the entry and the take-profit, then the take-profit itself. Without such a level, a third and two thirds of the way to the take-profit, then the take-profit.
  - **SL lock, per mode** (owner 2026-11-18, confirmed: "Setuju"; `ladderStep`, `LADDER_CONFIRM_TF`). The step is the mode the position opened in (recorded on its open intent with `r_dist`, its stop distance at entry). Scalp, or an unknown mode, uses `ladder_lock_pips` (30, a pip 0.1); intraday uses `ladder_r_intraday` (0.5) × R; swing uses `ladder_r_swing` (0.3) × R. One step past the entry, the stop goes to break-even plus the buffer (TP0); one step past TPx, it goes to TPx (owner 2026-11-18, in the owner's own words: "TP1+1 langkah geser ke TP1, TP2+1 langkah, geser ke TP2, dan seterusnya") (`ms:<ticket>:lad:<cents>`, reason `ladder_lock_tp<n>`). The trigger is the mode's last CLOSED broker candle, built from the M1 corpus (scalp M5, intraday M15, swing H1): its close must be beyond the level and the candle in the trade's direction (no reversal candle). With no such candle, or no R for an R mode, no new lock. The lock wins only when it is tighter than a stop already planned in the same sync, is never looser than the stop, and keeps the broker's stop distance. "The stop" is the tightest of the position's snapshot and the `modify_sl` intents of the last `STOP_COMMAND_MS` (5 minutes) still pending or sent, or done with an ok result (`commandedStops`, `withCommandedStop`): a snapshot taken before a modification landed never loosens it (2026-11-19, ticket 362785677: three modifications in seven seconds, 3719.19, 3719.23, 3719.19). The break-even lock is taken once: a stop already past the entry is not moved again at TP0, whose buffer follows the spread and the M5 ATR. Since SLG1 (2026-11-23) every ladder lock waits for the profit-lock gate, and TP0 is the gate's first lock (see the Position Manager section above).
  - **Partials.** At each rung reached, one partial close of the current volume, at most `ladder_max_partials` (3) a position and one per sync: `pl:<ticket>:<n>`, reason `ladder_tp<n>_<pct>pct`. The share follows the Decision Engine's power behind the position (fresh 35 minutes): strong `partial_min_pct` (25), weak or reversed `partial_max_pct` (50), otherwise between. The volume is floored to the broker's step and must leave at least the minimum open, so a 0.01 position gets the lock only. OmegaExecutor's `partial_close` (since 1.0) runs it; no EA change.
  - **Exposure only goes down:** no add, no wider stop. Under platform ADR-0020 Amendment 4 (merged 2026-11-18), the take-profit may move outward as often as the key levels allow (the trailing TP by key level, the extended target), but only while the stop already locks a profit at or beyond TP1 (`ladderTp1Locked`: the current stop or one planned in the same sync). Before that, an outward move is dropped. Knobs: `partial_min_pct` 10 to 50, `partial_max_pct` 25 to 75 (min not above max), `ladder_lock_pips` 5 to 100, `ladder_max_partials` 0 to 5.
- **Intents:** ids of at most 31 characters (the MT5 order comment); `pending` → `sent` (first returned) → `done` or `failed` by the bridge's result, or `expired` after 2 minutes (a late result still lands). An open is never retried. A close or kill still needed after its attempt ended gets the next attempt id (`<id>:<n>`, at most 10). The bridge never executes an id twice.
- **Page** (`nexus.cryptophys.work/omega/` and `omega.cryptophys.work/`): Broker connection, Engine (settings, Arm live), Bridge & terminal, Intents, Engine events. Every URL the page fetches or loads is relative. The static page at `omega.cryptophys.work/` is Omega's own fallback console (owner 2026-11-22, mockup approved): a status strip with the Nexus header's Omega codes from `./api/status` (`public/omega-status.js`, a plain-JS copy of `lib/headerStatus.ts` kept equal by `test/omega-page-status.test.mjs`), the EA heartbeat and the clock in UTC plus local time; a menu of its own sections (a drawer on phones); two absolute links to Nexus (`https://nexus.cryptophys.work/#/omega`, `#/`), its only absolute URLs (the old `../#/` links looped back to Omega itself there); a banner on a failed read; reads only while the tab is visible.
- **Engine form, Default** (owner 2026-11-20: "Follow the active profile"; `workers/xauusd-nexus-dashboard/ui/src/lib/engineDefaults.ts`). Setup → Engine's Default button fills the form only: risk, max positions and max daily loss from the connected account's profile (the overview's `accounts[].profile_limits`: `target_risk_pct`, `max_positions`, `daily_loss_pct` of `PROFILES`), max spread and signal age from `OMEGA_DEFAULTS` (the overview's `engine_defaults`); Conservative fills 2 / 2 / 6 / 60 / 10. No connected account, or a value the profile does not set (Custom): `OMEGA_DEFAULTS`. Save writes it through `POST /omega/api/settings` (audited); Default never touches `enabled`, `live_armed`, Arm or the profile. Under each field above what the profile applies, a note ("5% set, 2% applied (Conservative cap)"; the smaller of the two, as `effectiveLimits`).
- **Change audit on Telegram** (owner 2026-11-20: "harus ada notif Telegram karena buat mengetahui dan audit"; `src/change-notify.mjs`). Every audit row of a change that affects trading (`omega_events`: settings, the profile and its ceiling, agent mode and baseline, the trading mode, delegation, a hand modify or close, the connection, the bridge symbol) becomes a private line to the owner's chat: who (owner, an agent, Aether), old → new, the time; never an email, login, server or account reference. A switch saved several times in a row is one line per switch per minute with the value it ended on (KV `audit:notify:v1`: the cursor, the settings last told, the held changes). The sweep runs on the 5-minute ledger cron, on every 15-minute sweep while the market is closed, and right after every settings save; its first run starts at the newest row and sends nothing. Every `omega_events` action is in one list of `src/change-notify.mjs`: told by the sweep (`AUDIT_ACTIONS`), told where it happens (`TOLD_ELSEWHERE`), or not a change (`NOT_A_CHANGE`); a test fails on a new one. A line Telegram does not take keeps the cursor for the next sweep; after three sweeps in a row the cursor moves on and the health alert `audit_telegram_failed` says so. Identity keys and price keys are dropped by exact name. The EA control (pause, kill, resume) is told at once by `setControl`. The arm, probation and lens-floor lines stay where they were.
- **Entry council, first decision** (`src/limit-council.mjs` `firstCouncilLine`): the first decision in council mode is told once (KV `limit_council:first_council:v1`), with its side, mode, choice, rule and reason.
- **Fail closed on limit results** (owner 2026-11-20, the demo test skipped; `recordLimitFault` in `src/omega-broker.mjs`). A `place_limit`, `modify_order` or `delete_order` intent the EA never answered (it expires with no result) or answered as placed without a ticket, created after the owner's last save, while `entry_order` is limit or council, opens the limit fault: `omega_settings.limit_fault` (read with the settings the sync reads anyway: no query per sync), one `limit_fail_closed` audit row and one Telegram line, once per episode (the stop's own deletions that expire add nothing). While it is open the sync runs with `entry_order` market (resting Omega limits are deleted, `entry_order_market`) and the plan notes `limit_fail_closed`. The two faults differ (owner 2026-11-21, "Split"): **no ticket** → market entries until the owner saves the settings (`owner_saved_ms`; an Aether policy write or rollback does not clear it); **never answered** → the plan runs as paused (no new entry, no loosening; closes and protection still go out; a kill stays a kill) until the EA answers any intent again, or, when the EA was silent itself at the fault, until its heartbeat is fresh again at 1.21 or later (the next limit is the probe) (`clearNoAnswerFault`, one `limit_fault_cleared` row and one Telegram line), or the owner saves. While open, the health alert `limit_fail_closed` shows it with its age. A `wrangler secret put` creates a deployment with no message, which the deploy drift guard reads as drift and answers with a redeploy of `main` (harmless: secrets survive a deploy).

### Omega's policy from Aether (B-12, issue #227; owner 2026-11-11 "All")
- Hourly, the AI Worker pulls `AETHER.omegaPolicy()`: the doctrine-in-force values of `omega.risk_pct`, `omega.max_positions`, `omega.max_daily_loss_pct`, `omega.max_spread_points` and `omega.max_signal_age_min`. It stores them in KV `omega:policy` (`src/omega-policy.mjs`).
- **Off by default.** `applyOmegaPolicy` applies the pack only while `OMEGA_POLICY_AUTO` is `"true"` or the page's `policy_auto` is on.
- **Applying.** Each value is clamped to Omega's hard bounds (`OMEGA_POLICY_BOUNDS`). The change is audited in `omega_events` as `settings`, actor `aether`, and reported to Telegram. An unchanged pack does nothing.
- **Rollback.** After 50 Omega trades under a policy, it is reverted (actor `aether-rollback`, Telegram) when its profit factor is below 1 and below the 50 trades before it. Its doctrine sha is then never applied again.
- **Trade source.** Trades are told apart by `ea_deals.source` (`omega`, `manual`, `external`, `ea`; a column added 2026-11-11 from the bridge's deals).

### Corpus Oracle (owner 2026-11-11: "persiapkan engine baru bernama corpus oracle yang akan melakukan feeding data ke local lane dari live mt5 terminal sesuai dengan broker, pair dan user active dari omega"; `src/corpus-oracle.mjs`, docs/RFC_CORPUS_ORACLE.md)
- **Phase 1, the corpus** (`CORPUS_ORACLE_MODE = "shadow"` since 2026-11-11, owner: "Nyalakan, host sudah #243", collecting for the MT5 fallback of `docs/RFC_MT5_FALLBACK.md`; `"off"` ignores the bars). The Omega executor writes the broker's closed M1 bars of the symbol it trades (`omega/corpus.json`, UTC, no login), the bridge sends those after the server's cursor with each sync (`corpus`), and the AI Worker keeps a bar only from Omega's active broker (the terminal's server equals the stored connection's), pair (the executor's symbol) and user (the stored connection, its version recorded): D1 `corpus_bars` (14 days), `corpus_sources`; the sync answers `corpus_cursor_ms`.
- **Measured** at `GET /api/corpus/status`: freshness, missing minutes, complete 15m bars and the basis against the TradingView 15m series (mean offset broker minus TradingView, its spread, largest offset, return correlation).
- **Phase 2, the broker shadow lane** (2026-11-11). The pull adapter runs the 15m engines once more on the broker's M15 bars at each published 15m close (`broker-shadow.mjs`, `BROKER_SHADOW_MODE`). It sends only the field-by-field agreement with TradingView's records (`recordBrokerShadow`, D1 `broker_shadow`), shown under `shadow` on `GET /api/corpus/status`. It registers no identity and feeds no parity, room, decision or signal. It needs 400 broker M15 bars and is skipped while the MT5 fallback runs.
- **Feeds the MT5 fallback only** (since 2026-11-11, `docs/RFC_MT5_FALLBACK.md`, owner: trade as usual on the broker's raw price). While the TradingView gateway is down and the market trades, the pull adapter reads the active source's bars (`LocalComputationIngress.corpusBars`, `readCorpusBars`) and merges them into `OANDA:XAUUSD|1m` (`src: "mt5"`, `fallback.since_sec`); 5m, 15m and 1H aggregate from them (`FEED_FALLBACK_MODE = "auto"`, `market-data-ingestion/src/mt5-fallback.mjs`). Every local record, the 5m context and every decision built on them carry `feed: "mt5_fallback"`; parity skips those records (`fallback_skipped`, no approval earned or lost); the decision adds a caution and keeps its guardrails; the `market_data_fallback` alert and Telegram report the start and the end. When the gateway answers, native pulls over the fallback range (`healPlan`, once per 5 minutes) overwrite the broker bars and the derived series re-aggregate. Since 2026-11-11 (owner: "input untuk AI Worker bisa sama persis tidak ada regresi") the 4H and daily series are rebuilt too, on the grid of their last native bar (OANDA's session start, 22:00 UTC in winter; `aggregateCandles` `anchor`), and only for a bucket the 1m series covers from its start; the weekly bar waits for TradingView. **Stage 2** (2026-11-11, owner: no regression in the AI Worker's input): OmegaExecutor 1.10 to 1.12 also wrote (since 1.13 off by default, `CorpusMacroEnabled`, owner 2026-11-12: "data makro dimatikan di mt5": the broker supplies XAUUSD only) the broker's M1 bars of silver, oil and the dollar index's six pairs (`omega/corpus_macro.json`, inputs `CorpusMacroSymbols`, alternatives with `|`, the gold symbol's suffix tried first) and the USD economic calendar of MQL5 (`omega/calendar.json`, every 5 minutes); bridge 1.1.0 sends them after per-base cursors (`corpus_macro`, `calendar`; dropped first when a sync is too large); the AI Worker keeps them only from Omega's active broker and user (`ingestCorpusMacro`, sources `mt5macro:<server>:<BASE>`; `ingestMt5Calendar`, D1 `mt5_calendar`, 30 days).
